Heteroskedastic models are models where the variance of the error terms varies with the value of the independent variables. It means that the variables move together over time, and when adjusted for their own trends, the variables are said to be co-integrated. In other words, if two variables are co-integrated, they move together in a long-term equilibrium relationship, and any deviation from this relationship is temporary.
